## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ — ВИЖУ ПОЛНУЮ ЛОГИКУ

Теперь скопируем этот хук в ProjectsPage. Обновим `ProjectsPage.tsx`, добавив выполнение процессов:

```bash
nano tactical-command-v3/src/features/projects/ProjectsPage.tsx
```

В импорты добавь:

```tsx
import { executeProcess, getExecutionStatus } from '../process-designer/api/processApi';
import { websocketLogs } from '../../shared/services/websocketLogs';
```

Замени функцию `handleExecuteProcess` на:

```tsx
const handleExecuteProcess = async (processId: string, projectPath: string) => {
  console.log('Execute clicked:', processId, projectPath);
  eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `🚀 Executing process ${processId}...` });
  
  try {
    const result = await executeProcess(processId);
    const executionId = result.execution_id;
    
    eventBus.emit('process:started', { processId: executionId });
    
    // Подключаемся к WebSocket для получения логов
    websocketLogs.connect(executionId, (logData) => {
      console.log('[WebSocket] Log received:', logData);
      eventBus.emit('process:log', {
        source: 'process',
        message: `[${logData.type}] ${JSON.stringify(logData.data)}`
      });
    });
    
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `✅ Execution started: ${executionId}` });
    
    // Периодически проверяем статус
    const intervalId = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        const status = await getExecutionStatus(executionId);
        console.log('Status:', status);
        
        // Обновляем статусы узлов
        if (status.node_statuses) {
          Object.entries(status.node_statuses).forEach(([nodeId, nodeStatus]: [string, any]) => {
            const nodeStatusStr = typeof nodeStatus === 'string' ? nodeStatus : nodeStatus.status;
            if (nodeStatusStr === 'completed') {
              eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `✅ Node ${nodeId} completed` });
            } else if (nodeStatusStr === 'failed') {
              eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `❌ Node ${nodeId} failed: ${nodeStatus.result?.error || 'Unknown error'}` });
            }
          });
        }
        
        if (status.status === 'completed') {
          clearInterval(intervalId);
          websocketLogs.disconnect(executionId);
          eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `🏁 Process completed successfully!` });
          alert('Process completed successfully!');
        } else if (status.status === 'failed') {
          clearInterval(intervalId);
          websocketLogs.disconnect(executionId);
          eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `❌ Process failed: ${status.error || 'Unknown error'}` });
          alert(`Process failed: ${status.error || 'Unknown error'}`);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to get status:', err);
      }
    }, 1000);
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Execute error:', error);
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `❌ Error: ${error}` });
    alert(`Failed to execute process: ${error}`);
  }
};
```

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useFileStore } from './store/fileStore';
import { useDemoFileSystemProvider } from './hooks/useFileSystemProvider';
import { FileTree } from './components/FileTree/FileTree';
import { EditorPanel } from './components/EditorPanel/EditorPanel';
import { BottomPanel } from './components/bottom/BottomPanel';
import { TerminalInputStandalone } from './components/TerminalInputStandalone';
import { eventBus } from '../../shared/services/eventBus';
import { useResizePanels } from '../../hooks/useResizePanels';
import { listProjects, getProjectProcesses, executeProjectProcess, type Project, type Process } from './api/projectApi';
import '../process-designer/process-designer.css';


function SettingsPanel({ selectedProjectId }: { selectedProjectId: string | null }) {
  const { openFiles, activeFilePath } = useFileStore();
  const activeFile = activeFilePath ? openFiles.get(activeFilePath) : null;
  const [activeRightTab, setActiveRightTab] = useState('info');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [selectedProject, setSelectedProject] = useState<string | null>(selectedProjectId);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Загрузка проектов
  useEffect(() => {
    loadProjects();
  }, []);

  // Загрузка процессов при выборе проекта
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedProject) {
      loadProcesses(selectedProject);
    }
  }, [selectedProject]);

  const loadProjects = async () => {
    const projs = await listProjects();
    setProjects(projs);
    if (!selectedProject && projs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedProject(projs[0].id);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async (projectId: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    const procs = await getProjectProcesses(projectId);
    setProcesses(procs);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleExecuteProcess = async (processId: string, projectPath: string) => {
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `Executing process ${processId}...` });
    const result = await executeProjectProcess(processId, projectPath);
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `Result: ${JSON.stringify(result)}` });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const tabs = ['info', 'projects', 'processes', 'settings'];
    tabs.forEach(tab => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`${tab}Tab`);
      if (el) {
        el.style.display = tab === activeRightTab ? 'block' : 'none';
      }
    });
  }, [activeRightTab]);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="right-panel-tabs">
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'info' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('info')}
        >
          Info
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'projects' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('projects')}
        >
          Projects
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'processes' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('processes')}
        >
          Processes
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'settings' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('settings')}
        >
          Settings
        </button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="right-panel-content">
        {/* INFO TAB */}
        <div id="infoTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'info' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>FILE INFO</h4>
            {activeFile ? (
              <>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Name</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.path.split('/').pop() || ''} readOnly />
                </div>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Path</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.path} readOnly />
                </div>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Status</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.isDirty ? 'Modified' : 'Saved'} readOnly />
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="settings-field">
                <label>No file selected</label>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* PROJECTS TAB */}
        <div id="projectsTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'projects' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>PROJECTS</h4>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Select Project</label>
              <select 
                value={selectedProject || ''} 
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedProject(e.target.value)}
                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '6px', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid #3c3c3c', background: '#2d2d2d', color: '#ccc' }}
              >
                {projects.map(p => (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            {selectedProject && (
              <div className="settings-field">
                <label>Path</label>
                <input value={projects.find(p => p.id === selectedProject)?.source_path || ''} readOnly />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* PROCESSES TAB */}
        <div id="processesTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'processes' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>PROJECT PROCESSES</h4>
            {loading ? (
              <div className="settings-field">Loading...</div>
            ) : processes.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="processes-empty">
                <div className="settings-field">No processes for this project</div>
                <button 
                  className="project-select-btn"
                  onClick={() => {
                    eventBus.emit('process:create', { projectId: selectedProject });
                  }}
                >
                  Create Process
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="processes-list">
                {processes.map(p => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="process-item" style={{ 
                    padding: '8px', 
                    marginBottom: '8px', 
                    background: '#3c3c3c', 
                    borderRadius: '4px',
                    cursor: 'pointer'
                  }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: '4px' }}>{p.name}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#aaa', marginBottom: '8px' }}>{p.description}</div>
                    <button 
                      className="project-select-btn"
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                      onClick={() => handleExecuteProcess(p.id, projects.find(pr => pr.id === selectedProject)?.source_path || '')}
                    >
                      Execute
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SETTINGS TAB */}
        <div id="settingsTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'settings' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>SHORTCUTS</h4>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Save</label>
              <input value="⌘S / Ctrl+S" readOnly />
            </div>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Close Tab</label>
              <input value="⌘W / Ctrl+W" readOnly />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const { setProvider, initProject, root } = useFileStore();
  const provider = useDemoFileSystemProvider();
  const initialized = useRef(false);
  const { initResize } = useResizePanels();
  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const leftPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const rightPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const bottomPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialized.current && provider) {
      initialized.current = true;
      setProvider(provider);
      initProject('/my-project');
    }
  }, [provider, setProvider, initProject]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Слушаем событие создания процесса
    const handleCreateProcess = (data: any) => {
      console.log('Create process for project:', data.projectId);
      // TODO: Открыть Process Designer с предустановленным project_id
    };
    eventBus.on('process:create', handleCreateProcess);
    return () => {
      eventBus.off('process:create', handleCreateProcess);
    };
  }, []);

  // Инициализация ресайза
  useEffect(() => {
    if (leftPanelRef.current) {
      leftPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-right');
      leftPanelRef.current.style.width = '260px';
      leftPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '200';
      leftPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '400';
    }
    
    if (rightPanelRef.current) {
      rightPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-left');
      rightPanelRef.current.style.width = '280px';
      rightPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '240';
      rightPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '400';
    }
    
    if (bottomPanelRef.current) {
      bottomPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-top');
      bottomPanelRef.current.style.height = '250px';
      bottomPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '180';
      bottomPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '500';
    }

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      if (leftPanelRef.current && !leftPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-right')) {
        initResize(leftPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'right',
          minSize: 200,
          maxSize: 400,
          defaultValue: 260,
          cursor: 'ew-resize'
        });
      }
      
      if (rightPanelRef.current && !rightPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-left')) {
        initResize(rightPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'left',
          minSize: 240,
          maxSize: 400,
          defaultValue: 280,
          cursor: 'ew-resize'
        });
      }
      
      if (bottomPanelRef.current && !bottomPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-top')) {
        initResize(bottomPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'top',
          minSize: 180,
          maxSize: 500,
          defaultValue: 250,
          cursor: 'ns-resize'
        });
      }
    }, 100);
    
    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [initResize, root]);

  // WebSocket для получения логов
  useEffect(() => {
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
    
    const connectWebSocket = () => {
      try {
        ws = new WebSocket('ws://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/ws/logs');
        
        ws.onopen = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] ✅ WebSocket connected');
        };
        
        ws.onmessage = (event) => {
          try {
            const logData = JSON.parse(event.data);
            eventBus.emit('process:log', {
              source: logData.type || 'process',
              message: `[${logData.execution_id}] ${logData.type}: ${JSON.stringify(logData.data)}`
            });
          } catch (e) {}
        };
        
        ws.onerror = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] WebSocket server not available');
        };
        
        ws.onclose = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] 🔌 WebSocket disconnected');
          if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
          reconnectTimer = setTimeout(connectWebSocket, 5000);
        };
      } catch (e) {
        console.log('[ProjectsPage] WebSocket not supported');
      }
    };
    
    connectWebSocket();
    
    return () => {
      if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      if (ws) ws.close();
    };
  }, []);
  
  const handleCommand = (cmd: string) => {
    console.log('Command:', cmd);
  };
  
  // Управление табами правой панели
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleTabClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const tabButton = target.closest('.right-panel-tab');
      if (!tabButton) return;

      document.querySelectorAll('.right-panel-tab').forEach(tab => {
        tab.classList.remove('active');
      });
      tabButton.classList.add('active');
    };

    const container = document.querySelector('.right-panel-tabs');
    if (container) {
      container.addEventListener('click', handleTabClick);
    }

    return () => {
      if (container) {
        container.removeEventListener('click', handleTabClick);
      }
    };
  }, []);
  
  if (!root) {
    return (
      <div className="process-designer">
        <div className="loading">Loading project...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="process-designer">
      <div className="process-designer-main">
        <div className="node-palette-minimal" ref={leftPanelRef}>
          <div className="left-panel-header">
            <span>EXPLORER</span>
          </div>
          <div className="palette-content-minimal">
            <FileTree />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="canvas-terminal-container">
          <div className="process-designer-canvas" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            <EditorPanel />
          </div>
          
          <div className="terminal-panel" ref={bottomPanelRef} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '250px', minHeight: '180px' }}>
            <BottomPanel />
            <TerminalInputStandalone onCommand={handleCommand} />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="right-panel" ref={rightPanelRef}>
          <SettingsPanel selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

---

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ — ВОТ ОБНОВЛЕННЫЙ ФАЙЛ С РАБОТАЮЩИМ EXECUTE

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useFileStore } from './store/fileStore';
import { useDemoFileSystemProvider } from './hooks/useFileSystemProvider';
import { FileTree } from './components/FileTree/FileTree';
import { EditorPanel } from './components/EditorPanel/EditorPanel';
import { BottomPanel } from './components/bottom/BottomPanel';
import { TerminalInputStandalone } from './components/TerminalInputStandalone';
import { eventBus } from '../../shared/services/eventBus';
import { useResizePanels } from '../../hooks/useResizePanels';
import { listProjects, getProjectProcesses, type Project, type Process } from './api/projectApi';
import { executeProcess, getExecutionStatus } from '../process-designer/api/processApi';
import { websocketLogs } from '../../shared/services/websocketLogs';
import '../process-designer/process-designer.css';


function SettingsPanel({ selectedProjectId }: { selectedProjectId: string | null }) {
  const { openFiles, activeFilePath } = useFileStore();
  const activeFile = activeFilePath ? openFiles.get(activeFilePath) : null;
  const [activeRightTab, setActiveRightTab] = useState('info');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [selectedProject, setSelectedProject] = useState<string | null>(selectedProjectId);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [executingProcessId, setExecutingProcessId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Загрузка проектов
  useEffect(() => {
    loadProjects();
  }, []);

  // Загрузка процессов при выборе проекта
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedProject) {
      loadProcesses(selectedProject);
    }
  }, [selectedProject]);

  const loadProjects = async () => {
    const projs = await listProjects();
    setProjects(projs);
    if (!selectedProject && projs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedProject(projs[0].id);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async (projectId: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    const procs = await getProjectProcesses(projectId);
    setProcesses(procs);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleExecuteProcess = async (processId: string, projectPath: string) => {
    if (executingProcessId) {
      eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `⚠️ Already executing a process, please wait...` });
      return;
    }
    
    console.log('Execute clicked:', processId, projectPath);
    eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `🚀 Executing process ${processId}...` });
    setExecutingProcessId(processId);
    
    try {
      const result = await executeProcess(processId);
      const executionId = result.execution_id;
      
      eventBus.emit('process:started', { processId: executionId });
      eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `✅ Execution started: ${executionId}` });
      
      // Подключаемся к WebSocket для получения логов
      websocketLogs.connect(executionId, (logData) => {
        console.log('[WebSocket] Log received:', logData);
        eventBus.emit('process:log', {
          source: 'process',
          message: `[${logData.type}] ${JSON.stringify(logData.data)}`
        });
      });
      
      // Периодически проверяем статус
      const intervalId = setInterval(async () => {
        try {
          const status = await getExecutionStatus(executionId);
          console.log('Status:', status);
          
          // Обновляем статусы узлов
          if (status.node_statuses) {
            Object.entries(status.node_statuses).forEach(([nodeId, nodeStatus]: [string, any]) => {
              const nodeStatusStr = typeof nodeStatus === 'string' ? nodeStatus : nodeStatus.status;
              const nodeResult = typeof nodeStatus === 'object' ? nodeStatus.result : null;
              
              if (nodeStatusStr === 'completed' && nodeResult) {
                if (nodeResult.stdout) {
                  eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `✅ Node ${nodeId}: ${nodeResult.stdout}` });
                } else {
                  eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `✅ Node ${nodeId} completed` });
                }
              } else if (nodeStatusStr === 'failed') {
                const errorMsg = nodeResult?.error || 'Unknown error';
                eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'node', message: `❌ Node ${nodeId} failed: ${errorMsg}` });
              }
            });
          }
          
          if (status.status === 'completed') {
            clearInterval(intervalId);
            websocketLogs.disconnect(executionId);
            eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `🏁 Process completed successfully!` });
            setExecutingProcessId(null);
            alert('✅ Process completed successfully!');
          } else if (status.status === 'failed') {
            clearInterval(intervalId);
            websocketLogs.disconnect(executionId);
            eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `❌ Process failed: ${status.error || 'Unknown error'}` });
            setExecutingProcessId(null);
            alert(`❌ Process failed: ${status.error || 'Unknown error'}`);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to get status:', err);
        }
      }, 1000);
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Execute error:', error);
      eventBus.emit('process:log', { source: 'execute', message: `❌ Error: ${error}` });
      setExecutingProcessId(null);
      alert(`Failed to execute process: ${error}`);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const tabs = ['info', 'projects', 'processes', 'settings'];
    tabs.forEach(tab => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`${tab}Tab`);
      if (el) {
        el.style.display = tab === activeRightTab ? 'block' : 'none';
      }
    });
  }, [activeRightTab]);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="right-panel-tabs">
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'info' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('info')}
        >
          Info
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'projects' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('projects')}
        >
          Projects
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'processes' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('processes')}
        >
          Processes
        </button>
        <button 
          className={`right-panel-tab ${activeRightTab === 'settings' ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActiveRightTab('settings')}
        >
          Settings
        </button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="right-panel-content">
        {/* INFO TAB */}
        <div id="infoTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'info' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>FILE INFO</h4>
            {activeFile ? (
              <>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Name</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.path.split('/').pop() || ''} readOnly />
                </div>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Path</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.path} readOnly />
                </div>
                <div className="settings-field">
                  <label>Status</label>
                  <input value={activeFile.isDirty ? 'Modified' : 'Saved'} readOnly />
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="settings-field">
                <label>No file selected</label>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* PROJECTS TAB */}
        <div id="projectsTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'projects' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>PROJECTS</h4>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Select Project</label>
              <select 
                value={selectedProject || ''} 
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedProject(e.target.value)}
                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '6px', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid #3c3c3c', background: '#2d2d2d', color: '#ccc' }}
              >
                {projects.map(p => (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            {selectedProject && (
              <div className="settings-field">
                <label>Path</label>
                <input value={projects.find(p => p.id === selectedProject)?.source_path || ''} readOnly />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* PROCESSES TAB */}
        <div id="processesTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'processes' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>PROJECT PROCESSES</h4>
            {loading ? (
              <div className="settings-field">Loading...</div>
            ) : processes.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="processes-empty">
                <div className="settings-field">No processes for this project</div>
                <button 
                  className="project-select-btn"
                  onClick={() => {
                    eventBus.emit('process:create', { projectId: selectedProject });
                  }}
                >
                  Create Process
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="processes-list">
                {processes.map(p => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="process-item" style={{ 
                    padding: '8px', 
                    marginBottom: '8px', 
                    background: '#3c3c3c', 
                    borderRadius: '4px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    opacity: executingProcessId === p.id ? 0.6 : 1
                  }}>
                    <div style={{ fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: '4px' }}>{p.name}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#aaa', marginBottom: '8px' }}>{p.description}</div>
                    <button 
                      className="project-select-btn"
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                      onClick={() => handleExecuteProcess(p.id, projects.find(pr => pr.id === selectedProject)?.source_path || '')}
                      disabled={executingProcessId !== null}
                    >
                      {executingProcessId === p.id ? 'Executing...' : 'Execute'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SETTINGS TAB */}
        <div id="settingsTab" style={{ display: activeRightTab === 'settings' ? 'block' : 'none' }}>
          <div className="settings-section">
            <h4>SHORTCUTS</h4>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Save</label>
              <input value="⌘S / Ctrl+S" readOnly />
            </div>
            <div className="settings-field">
              <label>Close Tab</label>
              <input value="⌘W / Ctrl+W" readOnly />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const { setProvider, initProject, root } = useFileStore();
  const provider = useDemoFileSystemProvider();
  const initialized = useRef(false);
  const { initResize } = useResizePanels();
  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const leftPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const rightPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const bottomPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialized.current && provider) {
      initialized.current = true;
      setProvider(provider);
      initProject('/my-project');
    }
  }, [provider, setProvider, initProject]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Слушаем событие создания процесса
    const handleCreateProcess = (data: any) => {
      console.log('Create process for project:', data.projectId);
      // TODO: Открыть Process Designer с предустановленным project_id
    };
    eventBus.on('process:create', handleCreateProcess);
    return () => {
      eventBus.off('process:create', handleCreateProcess);
    };
  }, []);

  // Инициализация ресайза
  useEffect(() => {
    if (leftPanelRef.current) {
      leftPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-right');
      leftPanelRef.current.style.width = '260px';
      leftPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '200';
      leftPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '400';
    }
    
    if (rightPanelRef.current) {
      rightPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-left');
      rightPanelRef.current.style.width = '280px';
      rightPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '240';
      rightPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '400';
    }
    
    if (bottomPanelRef.current) {
      bottomPanelRef.current.classList.add('resizable-top');
      bottomPanelRef.current.style.height = '250px';
      bottomPanelRef.current.dataset.minSize = '180';
      bottomPanelRef.current.dataset.maxSize = '500';
    }

    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      if (leftPanelRef.current && !leftPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-right')) {
        initResize(leftPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'right',
          minSize: 200,
          maxSize: 400,
          defaultValue: 260,
          cursor: 'ew-resize'
        });
      }
      
      if (rightPanelRef.current && !rightPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-left')) {
        initResize(rightPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'left',
          minSize: 240,
          maxSize: 400,
          defaultValue: 280,
          cursor: 'ew-resize'
        });
      }
      
      if (bottomPanelRef.current && !bottomPanelRef.current.querySelector('.resize-handle-top')) {
        initResize(bottomPanelRef.current, {
          direction: 'top',
          minSize: 180,
          maxSize: 500,
          defaultValue: 250,
          cursor: 'ns-resize'
        });
      }
    }, 100);
    
    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [initResize, root]);

  // WebSocket для получения логов
  useEffect(() => {
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
    
    const connectWebSocket = () => {
      try {
        ws = new WebSocket('ws://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/ws/logs');
        
        ws.onopen = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] ✅ WebSocket connected');
        };
        
        ws.onmessage = (event) => {
          try {
            const logData = JSON.parse(event.data);
            eventBus.emit('process:log', {
              source: logData.type || 'process',
              message: `[${logData.execution_id}] ${logData.type}: ${JSON.stringify(logData.data)}`
            });
          } catch (e) {}
        };
        
        ws.onerror = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] WebSocket server not available');
        };
        
        ws.onclose = () => {
          console.log('[ProjectsPage] 🔌 WebSocket disconnected');
          if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
          reconnectTimer = setTimeout(connectWebSocket, 5000);
        };
      } catch (e) {
        console.log('[ProjectsPage] WebSocket not supported');
      }
    };
    
    connectWebSocket();
    
    return () => {
      if (reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      if (ws) ws.close();
    };
  }, []);
  
  const handleCommand = (cmd: string) => {
    console.log('Command:', cmd);
  };
  
  // Управление табами правой панели
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleTabClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const tabButton = target.closest('.right-panel-tab');
      if (!tabButton) return;

      document.querySelectorAll('.right-panel-tab').forEach(tab => {
        tab.classList.remove('active');
      });
      tabButton.classList.add('active');
    };

    const container = document.querySelector('.right-panel-tabs');
    if (container) {
      container.addEventListener('click', handleTabClick);
    }

    return () => {
      if (container) {
        container.removeEventListener('click', handleTabClick);
      }
    };
  }, []);
  
  if (!root) {
    return (
      <div className="process-designer">
        <div className="loading">Loading project...</div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="process-designer">
      <div className="process-designer-main">
        <div className="node-palette-minimal" ref={leftPanelRef}>
          <div className="left-panel-header">
            <span>EXPLORER</span>
          </div>
          <div className="palette-content-minimal">
            <FileTree />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="canvas-terminal-container">
          <div className="process-designer-canvas" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            <EditorPanel />
          </div>
          
          <div className="terminal-panel" ref={bottomPanelRef} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '250px', minHeight: '180px' }}>
            <BottomPanel />
            <TerminalInputStandalone onCommand={handleCommand} />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="right-panel" ref={rightPanelRef}>
          <SettingsPanel selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Сохрани файл и обнови страницу. Теперь кнопка Execute должна работать и показывать результат выполнения процесса.

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { TerminalHistoryOnly } from '../TerminalHistoryOnly';
import { ProblemsPanel } from './ProblemsPanel';
import { OutputPanel } from './OutputPanel';
import { DebugPanel } from './DebugPanel';

type TabType = 'terminal' | 'problems' | 'output' | 'debug';

export const BottomPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('terminal');

  const tabs = [
    { id: 'terminal' as const, label: 'Terminal' },
    { id: 'problems' as const, label: 'Problems' },
    { id: 'output' as const, label: 'Output' },
    { id: 'debug' as const, label: 'Debug' }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="projects-bottom-panel" style={{ 
      display: 'flex', 
      flexDirection: 'column', 
      height: '100%',
      overflow: 'hidden'
    }}>
      <div style={{ 
        display: 'flex', 
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)', 
        padding: '0 12px',
        flexShrink: 0,
        background: 'var(--k-bg-depth)',
        minHeight: '38.5px'
      }}>
        {tabs.map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab.id}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
            className={`bottom-tab ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            style={{
              padding: '8px 12px',
              background: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
              color: activeTab === tab.id ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: 400,
              letterSpacing: '0.5px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              borderBottom: activeTab === tab.id ? '2px solid var(--k-accent-ocean)' : '2px solid transparent',
              transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
              textTransform: 'none'
            }}
          >
            {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      <div style={{ 
        flex: 1, 
        overflow: 'auto',
        minHeight: 0,
        background: 'var(--k-bg-depth)'
      }}>
        {activeTab === 'terminal' && <TerminalHistoryOnly />}
        {activeTab === 'problems' && <ProblemsPanel />}
        {activeTab === 'output' && <OutputPanel />}
        {activeTab === 'debug' && <DebugPanel />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};%

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { eventBus } from '../../../../shared/services/eventBus';

interface OutputEntry {
  id: string;
  timestamp: Date;
  type: 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';
  source: string;
  message: string;
}

export const OutputPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const [outputs, setOutputs] = useState<OutputEntry[]>([]);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const outputEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleProcessLog = (data: any) => {
      const type = data.type || 'info';
      const newOutput: OutputEntry = {
        id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}-${performance.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date(),
        type: type as any,
        source: data.source || 'process',
        message: data.message
      };
      setOutputs(prev => [...prev, newOutput]);
    };

    eventBus.on('process:log', handleProcessLog);

    return () => {
      eventBus.off('process:log', handleProcessLog);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    outputEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [outputs]);

  const getTypeColor = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'success': return 'var(--k-status-success)';
      case 'warning': return 'var(--k-status-warning)';
      case 'error': return 'var(--k-status-error)';
      default: return 'var(--k-text-secondary)';
    }
  };

  const filteredOutputs = outputs.filter(out => filter === 'all' ? true : out.type === filter);

  const handleClear = () => {
    setOutputs([]);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ height: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{ 
        display: 'flex', 
        justifyContent: 'space-between', 
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '8px 12px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        flexShrink: 0
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
          {['all', 'info', 'success', 'warning', 'error'].map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f}
              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
              style={{
                padding: '4px 8px',
                fontSize: '11px',
                background: filter === f ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
                color: filter === f ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                border: `1px solid ${filter === f ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'var(--k-border)'}`,
                borderRadius: '4px',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                textTransform: 'uppercase'
              }}

Проблема: в `ProjectsPage` мы отправляем логи через `eventBus.emit('process:log', ...)`, но в `OutputPanel` они должны отображаться.

Проверим, есть ли в `ProjectsPage` импорт `OutputPanel` и используется ли он. В твоем `BottomPanel` есть `OutputPanel`, значит логи должны показываться во вкладке **Output** (не Terminal).

## Инструкция для тебя:

1. В правой нижней панели переключись на вкладку **"Output"** (не Terminal)
2. Нажми Execute для процесса
3. Логи должны появиться в Output

Если логов нет в Output, проверь консоль браузера на ошибки.

Также можешь добавить явную отправку логов в Output из `handleExecuteProcess`:

```tsx
// Добавь эту строку после получения статуса
eventBus.emit('process:log', { 
  source: 'execute', 
  type: 'info',
  message: `[${executionId}] ${status.status} - Node: ${status.current_node || 'none'}`
});
```

Покажи что видишь во вкладке Output после выполнения.

```bash
nano tactical-command-v3/src/features/projects/ProjectsPage.tsx
```

В компоненте `SettingsPanel`, в секции PROCESSES TAB, замени кнопку "Create Process" на:

## ШАГ 2 — Обновим ProcessDesignerPage для приема параметров проекта

```bash
nano tactical-command-v3/src/features/process-designer/ProcessDesignerPage.tsx
```

В начало файла, после импортов, добавь:

```tsx
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
```

Внутри компонента `ProcessDesignerPage`, добавь чтение параметров:

```tsx
export default function ProcessDesignerPage() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const projectIdFromUrl = searchParams.get('projectId');
  const projectPathFromUrl = searchParams.get('projectPath');
  
  const [currentProjectId, setCurrentProjectId] = useState<string | null>(projectIdFromUrl);
  const [currentProjectPath, setCurrentProjectPath] = useState<string | null>(projectPathFromUrl);
  
  // Слушаем событие создания процесса из проекта
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleCreateFromProject = (data: any) => {
      setCurrentProjectId(data.projectId);
      setCurrentProjectPath(data.projectPath);
    };
    eventBus.on('process:createFromProject', handleCreateFromProject);
    return () => {
      eventBus.off('process:createFromProject', handleCreateFromProject);
    };
  }, []);
```

## ШАГ 3 — Обновим API генерации для передачи project_id

В `processApi.ts` (или в месте вызова `design_process`), добавь передачу project контекста:

```bash
nano tactical-command-v3/src/features/process-designer/api/processApi.ts
```

Обнови функцию `designProcess`:

```tsx
export async function designProcess(requirement: string, projectId?: string, projectPath?: string): Promise<any> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/process/design`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ 
      requirement,
      project_id: projectId,
      project_path: projectPath
    })
  });
  return response.json();
}
```

## ШАГ 4 — Обновим бэкенд для приема project_id при генерации

```bash
nano ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_processes.py
```

Обнови класс `DesignRequest`:

```python
class DesignRequest(BaseModel):
    requirement: str
    project_id: Optional[str] = None
    project_path: Optional[str] = None
```

В эндпоинте `/design`, после генерации, если передан `project_id`, автоматически сохраняй процесс:

```python
@router.post("/design", response_model=DesignResponse)
async def design_process(request: DesignRequest):
    """Генерация процесса из текстового требования с опциональной привязкой к проекту"""
    try:
        designer = get_process_designer()
        result = await designer.design_process(request.requirement)
        
        # Нормализуем хэндлы
        if 'edges' in result and result['edges']:
            result['edges'] = normalize_handles(result['edges'])
        
        # Если указан project_id, автоматически сохраняем процесс
        if request.project_id:
            db = get_process_db()
            process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
            db.save_process(
                process_id=process_id,
                name=f"Generated: {request.requirement[:50]}",
                description=f"Auto-generated from: {request.requirement}",
                nodes=result['nodes'],
                edges=result['edges'],
                yaml=result.get('yaml', ''),
                mermaid=result.get('mermaid', ''),
                variables={},
                project_id=request.project_id,
                project_path=request.project_path
            )
            result['saved_process_id'] = process_id
            result['project_id'] = request.project_id
        
        # Убедимся, что у всех узлов есть type 'custom'
        if 'nodes' in result:
            for node in result['nodes']:
                if node.get('type') != 'custom':
                    node['type'] = 'custom'
        
        return DesignResponse(**result)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Design error: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))
```

Теперь обновим `handleSave` для передачи `project_id` и `project_path`:

Найди функцию `handleSave` и замени её на:

```tsx
const handleSave = async () => {
  addTerminalLog('[INFO] Saving process...');
  
  // Валидация перед сохранением
  const validation = validator.validate(nodes, edges);
  if (!validation.valid) {
    addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Cannot save: Invalid process structure');
    validation.errors.forEach(err => addTerminalLog(`  - ${err}`));
    alert('Cannot save: Invalid process structure\n\n' + validation.errors.join('\n'));
    return;
  }
  
  addTerminalLog('[OK] Process structure is valid');
  
  try {
    const processName = processList.find(p => p.id === currentProcessId)?.name || 'Untitled';
    const yaml = nodesToYaml(nodes, edges);
    const mermaid = nodesToMermaid(nodes, edges);
    
    if (!currentProcessId) {
      // Создаём новый процесс с project_id и project_path
      const result = await saveProcess(
        processName, 
        '', 
        nodes, 
        edges, 
        yaml, 
        mermaid,
        {}, // variables
        currentProjectId,    // project_id из URL
        currentProjectPath   // project_path из URL
      );
      setCurrentProcessId(result.id);
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Process saved with ID: ${result.id} (project: ${currentProjectId || 'none'})`);
    } else {
      // Обновляем существующий процесс
      await saveProcess(
        processName,
        '',
        nodes,
        edges,
        yaml,
        mermaid,
        {},
        currentProjectId,
        currentProjectPath
      );
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Process updated (project: ${currentProjectId || 'none'})`);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Save error: ${error}`);
    console.error('Save error:', error);
  }
};
```

const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process';

export interface ProcessNode {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  position: { x: number; y: number };
  data: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface ProcessEdge {
  id: string;
  source: string;
  target: string;
  animated?: boolean;
  style?: any;
}

export interface Process {
  id?: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  nodes: ProcessNode[];
  edges: ProcessEdge[];
  yaml?: string;
  mermaid?: string;
}

// Получить все типы узлов (плагины)
export async function getNodeTypes() {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/node-types`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch node types');
  return response.json();
}

// Генерация процесса через AI
export async function generateProcess(requirement: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/design`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ requirement })
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
  return response.json();
}

// Сохранение процесса
export async function saveProcess(name: string, description: string, nodes: ProcessNode[], edges: ProcessEdge[], yaml: string = '', mermaid: string = '') {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/save`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name, description, nodes, edges, yaml, mermaid })
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to save process');
  return response.json();
}

// Получить список процессов
export async function listProcesses() {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/list`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to list processes');
  return response.json();
}

// Получить процесс по ID
export async function getProcess(processId: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/${processId}`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to get process');
  return response.json();
}

// Запустить выполнение процесса
export async function executeProcess(processId: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/execute/${processId}`, {
    method: 'POST'
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to execute process');
  return response.json();
}
